# SOURCE_FILE: CHEY-2024-06-04_TotalExtraction_28655801.pdf
 
 
ORDINANCE NO. ________ 
 
ENTITLED: "AN 
ORDINANCE 
AMENDING 
SECTION 
10.16.150, 
PARKING 
VIOLATION HEARINGS AND APPEAL PROCEDURES, OF THE 
MUNICIPAL CODE OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE, WYOMING, 
MODIFYING 
THE 
PROCEDURES 
GOVERNING 
THE 
ADMINISTRATIVE REVIEW OF PARKING CITATIONS, MAKING 
CONFORMING AMENDMENTS AND REPEALING AN OBSOLETE 
PROVISION." 
 
 
BE IT ORDAINED BY THE GOVERNING BODY OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE, 
WYOMING: 
 
Section 1.  That Section 10.16.150, Parking Violation Administrative Review Procedures, 
of Chapter 10.16, Arrest and Citation Procedure, and Section 10.60.040, Fine Schedule, of Chapter 
10.60, Parking Limitations, of Title 10, Vehicles and Traffic, of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, 
are hereby amended to read: 
 
10.16.150 - Parking violation hearings and appeal administrative review procedures. 
 
A. Civil actions to enforce this chapter may be adjudicated by an administrative hearing 
officer actively licensed to practice law in the State of Wyoming designated by the mayor. 
 
A. Any person liable for a parking citation may request administrative review of the citation 
by contacting the police department parking administration manager in person, by 
telephone or mail not more than fifteen (15) days after the date of the issuance of the 
citation. The request shall specify the citation for which the person seeks review and 
provide the reasons for seeking administrative review. Under this section, a request for 
review made by telephone shall be considered received as of the day it was made if received 
by a police department employee between the hours of 8:00 a.m. and 5:00 p.m. of any day, 
except on Saturday, Sunday or a city holiday. 
 
B. A hearing for a parking violation may be heard by an administrative hearing officer. The 
administrative hearing officer may Upon receipt of a request for administrative review 
under subsection (A) of this section, the parking administration manager shall promptly 
transmit the request to the director of the compliance department or his or her designee. 
The director shall review the citation and the facts and circumstances surrounding the 
issuance of the citation and make such orders written determinations as may be necessary 
and proper to dispose of such cases to appropriately and impartially decide the controversy. 
, including reduction or increase of fine amounts notwithstanding the schedule of fines set 
forth by the governing body and this section. 
 
C. Any defendant who has been issued a parking citation Any person aggrieved by a the 
decision in a parking violation hearing of the director of the compliance department or his 
or her designee as provided in this subsection may seek judicial review in the manner 
8:31 am, Jun 18, 2024

 
Page 2 of 3 
 
provided by the Wyoming Administrative Procedure Act and the Wyoming Rules of 
Appellate Procedure. 
 
DC. 
If a defendant, who has been issued a parking citation, fails to request a hearing 
within fifteen (15) days after the issuance of the citation, the allegations in the citation Any 
person that fails to request a hearing within fifteen (15) days after the issuance of the 
citation as required under subsection (A) of this section shall be deemed to have waived 
their right to administrative review and to have admitted and to the allegations contained 
in the parking citation. After the expiration of the fifteen (15) day appeal period, the police 
department parking administration manager shall enter judgment for the city and impose a 
civil sanction in the amount of the issued citation and any fine increases in accordance with 
as provided by Section 10.16.090(A) of this chapter. 
 
E. 
If a defendant , who has requested a hearing in accordance with Section 10.16.140, fails to 
appear at their scheduled hearing, the police department parking administration manager shall 
enter judgment for the city and impose a civil sanction in the amount of the issued citation and any 
fine increases in accordance with Section 10.16.090 A. 
 
10.60.040 - Fine schedule. 
 
Any such fine to be imposed pursuant to a notice of violation issued under Section 10.60.030 
of this chapter, including any applicable administrative fees, shall be determined specified by 
resolution of the governing body, including any administrative fees, and shall be made available 
to the general public in the form of a fine schedule. Administrative fees shall be factored into the 
total amount of the fine before any late payment penalties are applied in accordance with Section 
10.16.090(A) of the city code. 
 
A. Administrative fees shall be factored into the total amount of the fine before any late 
payment penalties are applied in accordance with Section 10.16.090A. 
 
B. Court costs of ten dollars ($10.00) may be assessed against those requesting a hearing 
in accordance with Section 10.16.140 . 
 
Section 2.  That Section 10.16.140, Request for Hearing in Parking Cases, of Chapter 
10.16, Arrest and Citation Procedure, of Title 10, Vehicles and Traffic is repealed.  
 
10.16.140 - Request for hearing in parking cases. 
 
The person or persons liable for the parking violation may request a hearing for consideration 
of the citation by contacting the police department parking administration manager in person, by 
telephone or mail within fifteen (15) days after the date the citation was issued, payment of court 
costs of ten dollars ($10.00), and requesting a hearing to contest the alleged violation. A request 
for a hearing will be considered received by the city if it is actually received by the police 
department parking administration manager within fifteen (15) calendar days after the date the 
citation was issued. A request for a hearing made by telephone will be considered received the day 

 
Page 3 of 3 
 
it is made if it is received by a police department employee between the hours of 8:00 a.m. and 
5:00 p.m. of any day, except on Saturday, Sunday or a city holiday. 
 
 
Section 3.  Sections, paragraphs, sentences, clauses, and phrases of this ordinance are 
severable. If any section, paragraph, sentence, clause, or phrase of this ordinance is declared 
unconstitutional or otherwise unlawful, such declaration shall not affect any of the remaining 
sections, paragraphs, sentences, clauses, and phrases. 
 
 
Section 4.  This ordinance shall be in full force and effect upon approval and publication. 
 
 
FIRST READING:  
 
____________________________________ 
 
SECOND READING: 
 
____________________________________ 
 
THIRD AND FINAL READING: ____________________________________ 
 
 
 
__________________________________________ 
PATRICK COLLINS, MAYOR 
 
 
(SEAL)

_____________________________________

Published:_____________________

# SOURCE_FILE: CHEY-2024-06-04_TotalExtraction_95385062.pdf
 
ORDINANCE NO. ________ 
 
ENTITLED: "AN ORDINANCE CREATING SECTION 10.60.015, PAID ON-STREET 
PARKING DISTRICTS; ESTABLISHMENT; ADMINISTRATION, OF 
THE MUNICIPAL CODE OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE, WYOMING, 
ESTABLISHING THE PAID ON-STREET PARKING DISTRICTS 
PROGRAM 
AND 
MAKING 
CONFORMING 
AMENDMENTS 
AS 
SPECIFIED." 
 
 
BE IT ORDAINED BY THE GOVERNING BODY OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE, 
WYOMING: 
 
Section 1.  That Section 10.60.015, Paid On-Street Parking Districts; Establishment; 
Administration, of Chapter 10.60, Parking Limitations, of Title 10, Vehicles and Traffic, of the 
City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, is hereby created to read: 
 
10.60.015 - Paid on-street parking districts; establishment; administration. 
 
A. There is created the paid on-street parking districts program, which shall be administered 
by the parking division within the Cheyenne police department in accordance with this 
section. The geographic area of any paid on-street parking district administered under this 
program shall be designated by resolution of the governing body. 
 
B. Within each paid on-street parking district as designated by the governing body, the 
parking administration manager for the division shall establish appropriate on-street 
parking zones and reasonable on-street parking rates. The purpose for establishing any 
parking zone or parking rate shall be to positively influence parking behavior, prioritizing 
customer and visitor parking while emphasizing off-street options for longer-term parkers, 
such business employees and city residents. When establishing any parking zone or any 
parking rate under this subsection, the parking administration manager shall take the 
following factors into consideration: 
 
1. The types of activities or businesses that to be encompassed within the proposed zone 
and whether the proposed parking rate will support the efficient use of the available on-
street parking;  
 
2. Whether dynamic parking management is appropriate for a proposed zone in order 
manage demand for on-street parking through variable parking rates and dissemination 
of real-time parking availability information; and 
 
3. Other parking conditions, such as availability and usage trends, for the purpose of 
tailoring the paid on-street parking districts program to better match the specific needs 
and dynamics of a parking zone and between zones to optimize parking management 
and utilization. 
 
3:17 pm, Jun 10, 2024

 
Page 2 of 4 
 
C. Before any parking zone or parking rate is established under subsection (D) of this section, 
and before any parking zone or parking rate may be modified by the parking administration 
manager, the division shall provide public notice of the proposed parking zone or parking 
rate, or modification thereof, by means of publication of the notice on not less than one (1) 
City of Cheyenne media platform. In addition, the notice required under this subsection 
shall be provided for a period of not less than thirty (30) days before the proposed parking 
zone or parking rate establishment or modification is set to take effect and shall seek to 
solicit public input on the proposal. 
 
D. Every parking zone established under this section shall include appropriate signage 
communicating the type of zone and the authorized payment methods as provided by the 
division. Authorized payment methods may include payments through electronic means 
including using webpages, smartphone applications or payment kiosks. The division may 
require all persons wanting to park a vehicle within a paid on-street parking district to first 
register for a user account for the purposes of securing on-street parking payments. 
 
E. Information regarding every parking district and parking zone, along with all attendant 
parking rates, shall be made publicly available by the division. The information required 
under this subsection shall include maps for any parking district and parking zone 
established under this section and shall be publicly accessible on a City of Cheyenne media 
platform. 
 
F. As used in this section: 
 
1. "Division" means the parking division within the Cheyenne police department. 
 
2. "Paid on-street parking district" or "parking district" means the geographical area 
specified by resolution of the governing body for the implementation of paid on-street 
parking as provided by this section; 
 
3. "On-street parking zone" or "parking zone" means the geographical area located within 
a parking district that is specified in accordance with this section, which exhibits 
consistent usage characteristics that allow for the assessment of parking patterns and 
determination of parking rates based on parking demand; and 
 
4. "On-street parking rate" or "parking rate" means the monetary charge levied by the City 
of Cheyenne for the privilege of parking a vehicle for a specified duration in a 
designated parking space within any paid on-street parking district.  
 
Section 2.  That Section 10.04.020, Definitions, of Chapter 10.04, General Provisions, 
Section 10.52.220, Parking Time Limited, of Chapter 10.52, Stopping, Standing and Parking 
Generally, and Section 10.60.010, Signed Time Limitations, of Chapter 10.60, Parking 
Limitations, of Title 10, Vehicles and Traffic, of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, are hereby 
amended to read: 
 
 

 
Page 3 of 4 
 
10.04.020 - Definitions. 
 
The following definitions apply. Whenever any words and phrases are not defined in this code 
but are defined in state laws regulating the operation of vehicles, such a definition controls.  
 
A. Except as otherwise provided, as used in this title: 
 
1. "Alley" means a street or highway intended to provide access to the rear or side of lots 
or buildings in urban districts and not intended for the purpose of through vehicular 
traffic;. 
 
2. "Bicycle" means every device propelled by human power upon which any person may 
ride, having two tandem wheels either of which is more than fourteen (14) inches in 
diameter;. 
 
3. "Central business (or traffic) district" means all streets and portions of streets within 
the geographic area bounded by Fifteenth 15th Street on the south, Twentieth 20th 
Street on the north, Reed Street Avenue on the west and Evans Street Avenue on the 
east;. 
 
4. "Curb loading zone" means a space adjacent to a curb reserved for the exclusive use of 
vehicles during the loading or unloading of passengers or materials;. 
 
5. "Freight curb loading zone" means a space adjacent to a curb for the exclusive use of 
vehicles during the loading or unloading of freight or passenger;. 
 
6. "Motor vehicle" or "vehicle" means as defined by W.S. 31-1-101(a)(xv); 
 
7. "Passenger curb loading zone" means a place adjacent to a curb reserved for the 
exclusive use of vehicles during the loading or unloading of passengers;. 
 
8. "School zones" mean the streets and alleys and immediate intersections adjacent to any 
property used for school and for school play purposes;. and 
 
9. "Traffic division" means the traffic division of the police department of the city or, in 
the event a traffic division is not established, then the police department of the city. 
 
10.52.220 - Parking time limited. 
 
When signs are erected in each block giving notice thereof, no person shall park a vehicle for 
longer than the time specified on posted signs on any day except Sundays and public holidays, 
unless otherwise indicated upon any of the streets within the city as designated by the city traffic 
engineer. Any person who has parked a vehicle in an area where parking is limited by time shall 
before the expiration of that time move the vehicle as required by Section 10.60.010 of the city 

 
Page 4 of 4 
 
code. The requirements imposed under this section shall not apply to paid on-street parking 
districts under Section 10.60.015 of the city code. 
 
10.60.010 - Signed time limitations. 
 
Vehicles parked Any person who has parked a vehicle in an area where parking is limited by 
time must shall move from the block face of the vehicle's parked position before the expiration of 
the time indicated on posted signs. This requirement applies on any day except Sundays and public 
holidays, or unless otherwise indicated upon any of the streets within the city as designated by the 
city traffic engineer. The requirements imposed under this section shall not apply to paid on-street 
parking districts under Section 10.60.015 of this chapter. As used in this section, "block face" 
means the one (1) side of a specific city block where signed time limited parking is available.  
 
"Block face" is defined as one side of a specific city block where signed time limited parking 
is available.  
 
Section 3.  Sections, paragraphs, sentences, clauses, and phrases of this ordinance are 
severable. If any section, paragraph, sentence, clause, or phrase of this ordinance is declared 
unconstitutional or otherwise unlawful, such declaration shall not affect any of the remaining 
sections, paragraphs, sentences, clauses, and phrases. 
 
 
Section 4.  This ordinance shall be in full force and effect upon approval and publication.
